This CL may cause a small binary size increase, roughly proportional
to how long it's been since our last AFDO profile roll. For larger
increases (around or exceeding 100KB), please file go/crostc-bug.

Please note that, despite rolling to chrome/android, this profile is
used for both Linux and Android.

parent
066f93886d6b8c28972b63e1e5841ce0c8c94298 [ diff ] 
[Commerce] Add move operations to UrlInfo

commerce::UrlInfo declares its copy constructor, copy assignment
operator and destructor, which suppresses the implicit move operations.
Every std::move() of a UrlInfo therefore falls back to a deep copy of
its GURL and title.

ShoppingService::UpdateRecentlyViewedURL() runs on every tab switch or
focus change (OnWebWrapperSwitched()) and on every focused navigation
that updates history (OnWebWrapperViewed()). It erases the URL's
existing entry from recently_visited_tabs_ and inserts a new one at the
front, so std::vector shifts up to ten entries twice, copy-assigning
each of them. GetUrlInfosForRecentlyViewedWebWrappers() also copied
every entry twice: into its by-value loop variable and into the result.

This change:
- Declares noexcept, defaulted move operations for UrlInfo, so vector
  reordering and std::move() at call sites move instead of copy.
- Iterates recently_visited_tabs_ by const reference and reserves the
  result in GetUrlInfosForRecentlyViewedWebWrappers().
- Refreshes the title in place when the URL at the head of the list is
  viewed again, instead of erasing and re-inserting it. Entries only
  hold a URL and a title, so the resulting list is the same.

In a heap profile of Microsoft Edge, which shares this code,
UpdateRecentlyViewedURL() made 346 allocations (216 KB freed, 97 KB
outstanding). Nearly all of the outstanding memory was title and GURL
copies made by UrlInfo's copy assignment operator while
std::vector::insert() shifted the list. The list is capped at ten
entries, so this is churn rather than a leak, but it happens on the UI
thread on every tab switch.

Tested: static_asserts in shopping_service_unittest.cc check that
UrlInfo is nothrow move constructible and assignable, and the new
ShoppingServiceTest.TestRecentUrls_ViewingHeadAgainUpdatesTitle checks
that viewing the head URL again updates its title and keeps the order of
the list.

Bug: 568010589

683cfe6f53ddfe6439bfec9049c1342c6f753631 [ diff ] 
[ash] Remove expired HPS snooping protection and quick dim histograms

The ChromeOS.HPS.SnoopingProtection.* histograms,
ChromeOS.HPS.SnoopingProtectionNotificationSuppression.Enabled and
ChromeOS.HPS.QuickDim.Enabled expired in 2023 and 2024, so the code that
records them is dead. tools/metrics/histograms/README.md: "Once a
histogram has expired, the code that records it becomes dead code and
should be removed from the codebase."

Remove human_presence_metrics.h and the recording code, together with
the state that only existed for these metrics: the presence window
timestamp in SnoopingProtectionController, the pref read in
SnoopingProtectionNotificationBlocker::OnBlockingPrefChanged() and the
quick dim change tracking in PowerPrefs. PowerPrefs observes its prefs
with UpdatePowerPolicyFromPrefs() directly again, as it did before
https://crrev.com/c/3361618 added the quick dim metric. Also remove the
tests that only checked these histograms and their histograms.xml
entries. The other ChromeOS.HPS histograms are recorded outside of
Chromium and stay. No behavior change.

R=blundell@chromium.org

Bug: None

[Desktop Android] Implement site access options to install dialog

As part of Click-to-Script Phase 3a work, this CL implements the Desktop
Android plumbing to present users with site access permissions in the
extension installation dialog. The default selected option is "When you
click the extension”.

Demo video:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTQ1OTc1NjMzNTQ5NzIxNnw2NmI4M2Y3OS02Mw

This change is flag-guarded behind the
kExtensionInstallSiteAccessOptions flag. The exact UX styling will be
implemented in a follow-up CL.

Fixed: 568419086

86fed27a763c9de90017862c8e6ffa70e1570afe [ diff ] 
Refactor ONC validator valid-value helpers to use constexpr string_view arrays

Replace heap-allocated NoDestructor<std::vector<const char*>>
valid-value lists in chromeos/components/onc/onc_validator.cc with
compile-time constexpr arrays of std::string_view:

- Migrate ONC string constants from extern const char[] definitions in
  components/onc/onc_constants.cc to inline constexpr char[] definitions
  in components/onc/onc_constants.h so they can be used in constexpr
  std::string_view initializers.
- Refactor the GetValid*() and GetAllValid*() helper functions in
  onc_validator.cc to use static constexpr
  std::to_array<std::string_view>
  instead of base::NoDestructor<std::vector<const char*>>, returning
  base::span<const std::string_view>.
- Update Validator::IsValidValue,
  Validator::FieldExistsAndHasNoValidValue,
  and Validator::ListFieldContainsValidValues to accept
  base::span<const std::string_view>, calling base::JoinString directly
  and removing the JoinStringRange helper.
- Update Validator::ValidateClientCertFields,
  Validator::ValidateGlobalNetworkConfiguration, and Validator::ValidateEAP
  to use std::string_view containers and spans.

BYPASS_LARGE_CHANGE_WARNING: Trivial refactors to string constants.

Bug: none

[AnchoredPopupWindow] Introduce ViewBuilder for on-demand view creation

Allow AnchoredPopupWindow.Builder to construct views on demand using a
ViewBuilder functional interface that supplies AnchoredPopupWindow's
theme-wrapped Context (FillInContextThemeWrapper).

Clear cached mContentView upon dismissal so transient popups reconstruct
views freshly across theme and configuration changes. Retain a deprecated
Supplier<View> constructor overload for backward compatibility during caller
migration.

Bug: 568860935

fb11b50fed98c51b1898b13f76c1dbc1bc3e1597
parent
4bd74902bee1690651157fb94a6fdf8c244d05e4 [ diff ] 
media: Overlap origin ID fetch with MediaDrm construction

CL 8192077 introduced two-phase initialization in MediaDrmBridgeFactory,
decoupling MediaDrm pre-allocation from session opening and MediaCrypto
creation. This allowed Create() to return after MediaDrm construction
(3.82 ms JNI call), deferring openSession() to the storage callback.

Currently, the storage (origin ID) Mojo IPC is sent only after
CreateInternal() returns. Because CreateInternal() constructs MediaDrm
synchronously via JNI (~15-25 ms on Android), the construction and the
storage IPC run sequentially.

Send the storage request before constructing MediaDrm so that the Mojo
IPC round trip overlaps with CreateInternal(). The storage reply is
posted to the same thread and runs after media_drm_bridge_ is set. If
CreateInternal() fails, weak_factory_.InvalidateWeakPtrs() invalidates
the callback and the reply is dropped.

On a local Pixel 8a over 20 runs (n=20), this reduces createMediaKeys()
latency:
  - Median: 90.8 ms -> 77.9 ms (-12.9 ms / -14.2%)

Verified Widevine L3 (SW_SECURE_CRYPTO) video playback as well.

Bug: 542537288

Reviewed-by: Frank Liberato <liberato@chromium.org>

a034a70c605e39f12f35948a972332f2bd3e999d [ diff ] 
[Keyboard Shortcuts] Map missing keycodes in keyboard_code_conversion

We are migrating Clank's keyboard shortcut handling from
KeyboardShortcuts.java to C++ (ui::AcceleratorManagerAndroid +
chrome/browser/ui/accelerator_table.cc). Currently, several Android
keycodes used by Chrome shortcuts do not round-trip with their
corresponding ui::KeyboardCode in keyboard_code_conversion_android.cc:
1. Browser keys (AKEYCODE_REFRESH, AKEYCODE_SEARCH, AKEYCODE_BOOKMARK):
   - GetDomKeyFromAndroidKeycode() in this file already maps
     AKEYCODE_SEARCH -> DomKey::BROWSER_SEARCH and
     AKEYCODE_BOOKMARK -> DomKey::BROWSER_FAVORITES (and we add
     AKEYCODE_REFRESH -> DomKey::BROWSER_REFRESH), which correspond to
     VKEY_BROWSER_REFRESH, VKEY_BROWSER_SEARCH, and
     VKEY_BROWSER_FAVORITES in ui/events/keycodes/dom_us_layout_data.h
     and chrome/browser/ui/accelerator_table.cc.
   - However, KeyboardCodeFromAndroidKeyCode() only mapped AKEYCODE_BACK
     and AKEYCODE_FORWARD, returning VKEY_UNKNOWN for REFRESH, SEARCH,
     and BOOKMARK. Add these 3 mappings in both directions.
2. Plus key (AKEYCODE_PLUS -> VKEY_OEM_PLUS):
   - KeyboardShortcuts.java binds KeyEvent.KEYCODE_PLUS (Ctrl+Plus) for
     Zoom In, while accelerator_table.cc binds VKEY_OEM_PLUS. Map
     AKEYCODE_PLUS to VKEY_OEM_PLUS alongside AKEYCODE_EQUALS.
3. Page Up / Page Down (VKEY_PRIOR / VKEY_NEXT):
   - In KeyboardCodeFromAndroidKeyCode() (Android -> Chrome), both
     AKEYCODE_PAGE_UP/DOWN and TV remote AKEYCODE_CHANNEL_UP/DOWN map to
     VKEY_PRIOR/VKEY_NEXT.
   - However, in the reverse function AndroidKeyCodeFromKeyboardCode()
     (Chrome -> Android), VKEY_PRIOR and VKEY_NEXT were returning
     AKEYCODE_CHANNEL_UP and AKEYCODE_CHANNEL_DOWN instead of
     AKEYCODE_PAGE_UP and AKEYCODE_PAGE_DOWN. Update them to return
     AKEYCODE_PAGE_UP and AKEYCODE_PAGE_DOWN.

Bug: 567230357

Return EditContext selection bounds from TextInputManager::GetSelectionRegion
📋 Metadata
commit
ad9052be7f3e4b295a5a15c2a52b6439a7b87334
[ log ]
author

tree
5ffd998f6c3809f8adcdaa8cf04443b64986e2a0
parent
d84e3b80d52c26d4eccf6dd75b57dedb7046bf6a [ diff ] 
Return EditContext selection bounds from TextInputManager::GetSelectionRegion

TextInputManager::GetSelectionRegion() and OnSelectionBoundsChanged()
only consider the DOM selection reported via SelectionBoundsChanged.
For EditContext-based editors (e.g. Google Docs), the DOM selection
often lives in a hidden element (Docs parks it in an iframe at
top:-10000px), while the rendered caret/selection bounds are supplied
by the page via EditContext.updateSelectionBounds() and arrive in
TextInputState::edit_context_selection_bounds. As a result, callers of
GetSelectionRegion() and OnSelectionBoundsChanged() (such as
WebContents::GetFocusSelectionBounds(), GetTextSelectionBounds(), and
RegisterFocusSelectionBoundsChanged(), used by the Dictation / Voice
Typing overlay) see stale or off-screen bounds in EditContext editors.

This change:
- Tracks a parallel ViewMap<std::optional<SelectionRegion>>
  edit_context_selection_region_map_ in TextInputManager, updated in
  UpdateTextInputState() from non-empty EditContext selection bounds and
  returned from GetSelectionRegion() before falling back to
  selection_region_map_, so DOM selection updates do not overwrite
  EditContext bounds and vice versa.
- Notifies OnSelectionBoundsChanged() observers when the active view's
  EditContext SelectionRegion changes.

Fuchsia-Binary-Size: Size increase is unavoidable.
Bug: b:568057871
Change-Id: I9657f435c05dfe1f427e396be7fde3fad8850337

Reviewed-by: Dave Tapuska <dtapuska@chromium.org>
Commit-Queue: Gary Klassen <gklassen@chromium.org>

6c6cfd43c19cf8951b17f191fde7fbaf5529ca06 [ diff ] 
Enable exit-time destructor warnings for crash and media controls

Remove no_exit_time_destructors from //components/crash/core/app and
//components/global_media_controls.

- global_media_controls: build the timestamp FontList where it's used
  instead of keeping a global.
- crashpad_mac.mm and crashpad_ios.mm: cache the process annotations in
  a base::NoDestructor. They are computed once from bundle lookups and
  read on every crash report, so per-call rebuilding isn't wanted.

91326104c9290ebc09b8b1994d76274afaf465eb [ diff ] 
Move global PackageIds into app service test fixtures

apps::PackageId has a non-trivial destructor and no constexpr
constructor, so each namespace-scope PackageId constant is an exit-time
destructor. They compile today only because these targets still carry
no_exit_time_destructors. Make them fixture members instead, as a step
toward removing that exemption.

TAG=agy
CONV=24fb0bac-49f1-4a9f-9459-e507cfd2dbfd

Bug: 40031409, 522379416

Fix race in CancelFlowOnAccountRemoval browser test

In
ProfileManagementDisclaimerServiceBrowserTest.CancelFlowOnAccountRemoval,
a RunLoop::RunUntilIdle() runs after creating the profile management
disclaimer dialog before
handler->SetJavaScriptAllowedCallbackForTesting() is registered. If the
WebUI page finishes initializing during that RunLoop, AllowJavascript()
has already been called on ManagedUserProfileNoticeHandler before the
test registers its callback. Because
WebUIMessageHandler::AllowJavascript() only invokes
OnJavascriptAllowed() on the transition to allowed, the callback is
never invoked and javascript_allowed_future.Wait() times out.

Update
ManagedUserProfileNoticeHandler::SetJavaScriptAllowedCallbackForTesting
to invoke the callback immediately if IsJavascriptAllowed() is already
true.

Bug: 543381833

ios: Support Linux host in Apple GN rules and SDK tools

Update Apple GN configurations and Python build scripts to support
cross-compiling iOS targets from a Linux host:
- Read Xcode and SDK version plists directly in `sdk_info.py` on
  non-Darwin hosts and create relative SDK symlinks, with unit tests
  and `build/config/apple/PRESUBMIT.py`.
- Skip codesigning on non-Darwin hosts (logging a notice for non-adhoc
  identities), extract provisioning profile XML plists directly, and
  parse Mach-O headers and strip Fat `arm64e` slices in Python on
  non-Darwin hosts in `strip_arm64e.py`.
- Disable Clang modules, codesigning, and `iossim` dependencies when
  building iOS targets on a non-Mac host.
- Use the host CIPD `gperf` binary for Blink scripts when `host_os !=
  "mac"`.

[Autofill] Preserve BAM popup vector index in FormSuggestionMetadata
📋 Metadata

917f4570868e22287713d695871fe58dc4de008c
[ log ]

tree
2f02bf0da9f460d845806aee28c3270e912f3ffc
parent
3c9a775dbd2923d3e657cc9d78caafcfe03a2cf2 [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Preserve BAM popup vector index in FormSuggestionMetadata

Follows the approach used on Android.

BrowserAutofillManager (BAM) expects DidAcceptSuggestion() to receive
the index corresponding to its original suggestion vector rather than
the UI array index. On iOS, AutofillAgent may reorder or filter
suggestions before rendering them (e.g. prepending SuggestionType::kUndo
to the front of the Keyboard Accessory suggestion list).

Passing the UI array index when suggestions are reordered caused an
off-by-one contract mismatch, shifting accepted suggestion indices in:
1. UKM FormInteractionsUkmLogger::LogSuggestionAccepted (corrupting
logged suggestion rows). 2. UMA Autofill.SuggestionAcceptedIndex.*
metrics (corrupting acceptance ranking telemetry).

This CL adds `popup_suggestion_index` to `FormSuggestionMetadata` to
store the original BAM suggestion vector index upon receiving
suggestions in `showAutofillPopup:`, and passes it to
`DidAcceptSuggestion()` when selected.

Bug: 568049725

cf098eeefcd5aca3361bba7216c1f704592acfd8 [ diff ] 
ash-settings: Modernize property declarations and observer receivers

Add declare keyword to Polymer properties in mixins and pages, use
protected visibility for template-bound properties, construct Mojo
observer receivers inline, and add trailing underscores to private
members in preparation for enabling strictPropertyInitialization
across Polymer ChromeOS.

Additionally, move observer binding logic in
per_device_keyboard_subsection from connectedCallback() to the
constructor to match per_device_touchpad_subsection and device_page,
preventing crashes from re-binding inline-constructed receivers if
the element is detached and re-attached to the DOM.

Bug: None
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*OSSettings*

tree
6394284c5320d82746d118939d8b88284437715d
parent
504acb739ebc52a8056ea15c7de444851c1e808f [ diff ] 
[iOS] Fix OverlayPresentationContextImpl re-entrancy on coordinator stop

When stopping OverlayContainerCoordinator while a contained overlay is
presented, SetDelegate(nil) resets presentation capabilities to kNone.
Previously:
1. OverlayPresentationContextImpl::SetDelegate called
   UpdatePresentationCapabilities() before updating delegate_ to nil.
2. OverlayPresenterImpl::
   OverlayPresentationContextDidChangePresentationCapabilities called
   PresentOverlayForActiveRequest() even when CanShowUIForRequest() was
   false, which called PrepareToShowOverlayUI(request) and asked the
   stopping OverlayContainerCoordinator to re-enable kContained
   capabilities, re-entering UpdatePresentationCapabilities() and
   triggering a CHECK in base::ObserverList.

Update OverlayPresentationContextDidChangePresentationCapabilities to
only call PresentOverlayForActiveRequest() when CanShowUIForRequest() is
true, set delegate_ before resetting presentation capabilities in
SetDelegate(), and mark OverlayContainerCoordinator as stopped at the
beginning of -stop.

Change-Id: I28fc7a8177283f8573b6adad6e7894f828b3388d
Bug: 557137643
Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8459487
Commit-Queue: Federica Germinario <fedegermi@google.com>
Reviewed-by: Mike Dougherty <michaeldo@chromium.org>
Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1710694}
